        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Wan2GP Fork Migration — Sprint 2

## Overview

Sprint 2 replaces `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` (a 1400-file flat-copied tree, currently tracked in reigh-worker git) with a git submodule mounted at the same path, pointing at a branch on `banodoco/Wan2GP` whose working tree matches current banodoco behavior everywhere **except** the explicitly-resynced surfaces. Four concrete outputs:

1. Submodule cutover that preserves the SD-001 path contract (absolute `Wan2GP/` on `sys.path`, bare-module callers still resolve).
2. `shared/` + `postprocessing/` resync honoring `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` bucket decisions.
3. `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py` introduced from upstream HEAD (available-only, not promoted to default per SD-005). `Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` aligned to upstream HEAD.
4. Stale-doc wording removed from `README.md:39`, `STRUCTURE.md:22`, `STRUCTURE.md:70`, `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202`.

Sprint 2 must NOT touch existing files under `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/`. `res2s.py` is a new file added to that tree, which is consistent because the non-negotiable is about modifying existing ltx2 behavior, not about the directory being frozen. No behavior change outside `shared/` + `postprocessing/` drift classified as bucket (a).

Fork construction strategy: build an integration branch `reigh-sprint-2` on `banodoco/Wan2GP` whose tree equals `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` everywhere (preserving all current banodoco drift — defaults, models/wan/, models/ltx2/, loras_qwen/, profiles/, plugins/, etc.) EXCEPT where Sprint 2 is explicitly resyncing. The submodule then pins to that branch tip SHA. This keeps non-Sprint-2 surfaces byte-identical to pre-cutover, and centralizes Sprint 2's tiny actual delta into the `shared/` + `postprocessing/` + self_refiner + res2s changes.

## Phase 0: Preflight

### Step 1: Record baseline tag and capture pre-cutover fingerprint
**Scope:** Small
1. **Verify** working tree is clean (expected untracked only: `docs/WAN2GP_FORK_MIGRATION_PLAN.md`, `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv`, `scripts/live_test/`). Confirm HEAD is current `main`.
2. **Create annotated tag** `wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline` on `reigh-worker` HEAD: `git tag -a wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline -m "Sprint 2 baseline"`. Do NOT push yet — tag lives locally until Sprint 2 lands.
3. **Fingerprint current Wan2GP tree** for post-cutover byte-compare verification: `git ls-tree -r HEAD Wan2GP | sha256sum > /tmp/wan2gp_pre_cutover.sha256` and capture tree SHAs for `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/` and `Wan2GP/models/qwen/` specifically for the non-regression assertion.
4. **Verify fork access**: confirm `banodoco/Wan2GP` is reachable and capture banodoco `main` tip SHA + upstream HEAD SHA.

## Phase 1: Construct the `reigh-sprint-2` fork branch

### Step 2: Clone the banodoco fork into a scratch directory (`/tmp/wan2gp-fork/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Clone**: `git clone https://github.com/banodoco/Wan2GP.git /tmp/wan2gp-fork` and `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork fetch origin main`.
2. **Branch from `main`**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork checkout -b reigh-sprint-2 origin/main`. The starting point is upstream HEAD (Sprint 1 state).
3. **Confirm** tip SHA matches Sprint 1's fork cut.

### Step 3: Mirror current banodoco vendored tree onto `reigh-sprint-2` (everything-carry commit)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Compute the target content** for the fork branch: it must equal `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` as-of baseline. This preserves all current banodoco drift — defaults, `models/ltx2/` (hot zone, not touched until Sprint 3), `models/wan/`, `models/qwen/`, `loras_qwen/`, `profiles/`, `plugins/`, `preprocessing/`, `shared/`, `postprocessing/`, top-level `pyproject.toml`/`requirements.txt`, etc.
2. **Rsync-style copy**: from `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` into `/tmp/wan2gp-fork/`, excluding `.git*` and any ignored artifacts.
3. **Stage + review diff**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork add -A` then `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork status` and spot-check that the staged changes match the ~22 `models/ltx2/` file diffs, banodoco-only files in `defaults/`, `loras_qwen/`, etc., and expected bucket (b)+(c) drift everywhere else.
4. **Commit**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork commit -m "banodoco carry: mirror reigh-worker/Wan2GP baseline onto fork"`. This single commit converts the fork branch from upstream-HEAD-only to banodoco-baseline-equivalent.

### Step 4: Apply Sprint 2 resync delta on top of the carry commit
**Scope:** Medium
1. **`shared/` triage application**: for every row in `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` with path under `Wan2GP/shared/`:
   - **Bucket (a) `accept-upstream`**: revert to upstream content via `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork checkout origin/main -- <path>` (these are files where banodoco had drift we now accept back to upstream).
   - **Bucket (b) `upstreamable`**: leave as carry commit (banodoco content preserved). No action.
   - **Bucket (c) `banodoco-fork-or-banodoco-patch`**: leave as carry commit.
   - **Bucket (d) `cruft`**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork rm <path>`. (None expected in `shared/` per current CSV; verify.)
2. **`postprocessing/` triage application**: current CSV has no `postprocessing/` rows, implying no drift vs upstream. Verify `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork diff origin/main -- postprocessing/` is empty. If non-empty, flag and treat each divergence as bucket (a) resync unless reviewer directs otherwise.
3. **`shared/utils/self_refiner.py` alignment**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork checkout origin/main -- shared/utils/self_refiner.py`. Plan §2c treats this as drift-only resync. Stage.
4. **`models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py` introduction**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork checkout origin/main -- models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py`. CSV row 110 — upstream-only addition, bucket (a). No wiring change: the file is a sampler implementation only invoked when a defaults/profile references `res_2s`. `HQ (Res2s).json` preset already exists (CSV row 195). SD-005 gating is satisfied because no defaults file is modified to make `res_2s` the default sampler.
5. **Commit the Sprint 2 delta**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork commit -m "sprint-2: resync shared/ + postprocessing/, introduce res2s, align self_refiner"`. Verify the commit diff is confined to `shared/`, `postprocessing/`, and `models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py`.
6. **Push** `reigh-sprint-2` to `banodoco/Wan2GP` (confirm with user before pushing since this is visible-to-banodoco).
7. **Capture tip SHA** for the submodule pointer.

## Phase 2: Reigh-worker submodule cutover

### Step 5: Replace flat `Wan2GP/` tree with submodule (`reigh-worker/`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Remove tracked flat tree**: `git rm -r --cached Wan2GP/` (removes from index, leaves working copy). Then `rm -rf Wan2GP/`.
2. **Add submodule**: `git submodule add -b reigh-sprint-2 https://github.com/banodoco/Wan2GP.git Wan2GP`. Creates `.gitmodules`, populates `.git/modules/Wan2GP/`, checks out the branch tip into `Wan2GP/`.
3. **Pin the submodule pointer** to the specific SHA from Step 4.7: `git -C Wan2GP checkout <sha>` then `git add Wan2GP .gitmodules`.
4. **Verify `.gitmodules`**:
   ```
   [submodule "Wan2GP"]
       path = Wan2GP
       url = https://github.com/banodoco/Wan2GP.git
       branch = reigh-sprint-2
   ```
5. **Verify submodule health**: `git submodule status` shows `Wan2GP` at the pinned SHA with no `+`/`-` prefix.
6. **Do not commit yet** — run validation gates first.

### Step 6: Validate path-contract smoke (strict path smoke)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the strict path-contract one-liner from the repo root:
   ```bash
   python3 -c "import os, sys; from source.core.runtime_paths import ensure_wan2gp_on_path, get_wan2gp_path; ensure_wan2gp_on_path(); assert os.path.abspath(str(get_wan2gp_path())) in [os.path.abspath(p) for p in sys.path]; assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_handler.py'); assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_main.py'); assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/shared/utils/loras_mutipliers.py'); assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py'); assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py'); print('path-contract smoke OK')"
   ```
2. **Must exit 0** with `path-contract smoke OK`. If any assertion fails, pause cutover and diagnose (most likely: fork branch missing one of the five listed files).

### Step 7: Run the 6-file travel regression suite
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run**:
   ```bash
   uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py
   ```
2. **Must be all-green**. If any test fails, inspect whether the resynced `shared/` file touches code on the travel path. If so, reclassify the triggering file to bucket (c) on the fork branch (carry banodoco drift), amend Phase 1 Step 4, re-push, and re-pin.

## Phase 3: Doc updates

### Step 8: Rewrite stale vendored-subtree wording (`reigh-worker/README.md`, `reigh-worker/STRUCTURE.md`, `reigh-worker/docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **`README.md:39`** — replace the "vendored upstream input ... subtree" sentence with submodule-accurate language describing pointer bumps, with guidance that upstream requirement changes are mirrored into root worker metadata before regenerating `uv.lock`.
2. **`README.md:103`** (Powered By section) — if present, correct "the `Wan2GP/` directory contains the upstream engine" to note the submodule pinned to `banodoco/Wan2GP`.
3. **`STRUCTURE.md:22`** — replace `vendored upstream. Dependency drift ...` with submodule-accurate language describing pointer bumps rather than subtree edits.
4. **`STRUCTURE.md:70`** — replace `Upstream video generation engine (vendored, DO NOT MODIFY)` with `Git submodule pointing at banodoco/Wan2GP; edit via fork PR + pointer bump, not in-place`.
5. **`docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202`** — replace `still a vendored upstream subtree` with submodule language; preserve the paragraph's existing guidance about mirroring requirements into root worker metadata.

### Step 9: Doc-sweep gate (`reigh-worker/docs/`, `reigh-worker/README.md`, `reigh-worker/STRUCTURE.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Narrow sweep must return zero hits**:
   ```bash
   rg "vendored.*subtree" docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md
   ```
2. **Broader sweep must return zero hits**:
   ```bash
   rg -l 'Wan2GP' docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md | xargs -I{} rg -H -n 'DO NOT MODIFY' {}
   ```
3. Note: hits on `docs/WAN2GP_FORK_MIGRATION_PLAN.md` for the first regex are historical prose describing the pre-Sprint-2 state; those are expected to remain (the plan doc is not among the stale-doc update targets and its narrative is about what *was* true). Confirm with the reviewer that those hits are acceptable — if not, reword those prose lines.

## Phase 4: Land

### Step 10: Commit the submodule cutover + doc updates
**Scope:** Small
1. **Stage**: `.gitmodules`, `Wan2GP` (the gitlink), all deletions under `Wan2GP/`, `README.md`, `STRUCTURE.md`, `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md`.
2. **Commit** with a message like `chore: replace vendored Wan2GP tree with submodule at reigh-sprint-2`. Reference the baseline tag in the body.
3. **Do not push without explicit user confirmation** — the submodule commit permanently alters the repo shape.

## Execution Order

1. Preflight and baseline tag first (Step 1) — this is the rollback anchor.
2. Build the fork branch completely offline in a scratch clone (Steps 2–4), push only after local sanity checks.
3. Cut reigh-worker over to the submodule (Step 5), then gate on the strict path smoke (Step 6) BEFORE running pytest.
4. Run the 6-file travel suite (Step 7).
5. Update docs (Step 8) and gate doc-sweep (Step 9).
6. Commit (Step 10).

## Validation Order

1. Cheapest: strict path-contract smoke (Step 6) — instant feedback on the SD-001 invariant.
2. Fast: doc-sweep regex sweeps (Step 9).
3. Slowest: 6-file travel pytest suite (Step 7).

If any of (A) strict path smoke, (B) 6-file suite, (C) doc-sweep gate fails, roll back:
```bash
git submodule deinit Wan2GP && git reset --hard wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline
```
Rollback leaves the banodoco `reigh-sprint-2` branch in place (no harm); push can be retried after fixing the failing gate.

    "`postprocessing/` has zero drift vs upstream (no rows in the triage CSV), so bucket-wise resync of postprocessing/ is a no-op; any surprise divergence found during Step 4.2 gets flagged.",
    "`shared/utils/self_refiner.py` is treated as bucket (a) resync to upstream HEAD based on plan \u00a72c guidance, since the triage CSV has no row for it.",
    "The rollback command `git submodule deinit Wan2GP && git reset --hard wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline` is the authoritative rollback; the banodoco `reigh-sprint-2` branch stays in place after rollback (no force-delete) so a subsequent retry does not have to rebuild it.",
    "CSV's 269 content rows are exhaustive for Sprint 2 triage decisions in shared/ + postprocessing/; no additional classification work is needed in Sprint 2.",
    "Sprint 2 does not push reigh-worker commits to `origin/main`; all pushes happen only after explicit user confirmation per the task's scope (create the commit locally + pushable-but-not-pushed)."
  ],
  "structure_warnings": []
}

  "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-007",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-008",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-018",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-106",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-107",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "id": "DEBT-013",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the package is internally inconsistent. the plan body says to keep `activepairdata` in `usestate` and make phase 2 optional, but the attached metadata and success criteria still describe the previous derive-via-`usememo` / remove-`setactivepairdata` / simplify-`onpairclick` plan. that means the approved-plan requirements are only partially coherent as presented.",

      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-108",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,

        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }

      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",

    {
      "id": "DEBT-110",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-109",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],

  {
    "subsystem": "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct",
    "total_occurrences": 5,
    "plan_count": 2,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-007",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-008",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-018",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-106",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-107",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Wan2GP Fork Migration — Sprint 2 (Revised)

## Overview

Sprint 2 replaces `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` (a 1400-file flat-copied tree, currently tracked in reigh-worker git) with a git submodule mounted at the same path, pointing at an integration branch on `banodoco/Wan2GP`. Four concrete outputs:

1. Submodule cutover that preserves the SD-001 path contract. Keeping `Wan2GP/` on `sys.path` resolves **all** bare-module imports in the vendored runtime — the three named in `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py:76-88` (`models.qwen.qwen_handler`, `shared.utils.loras_mutipliers`, `models.qwen.qwen_main`) plus every other tree-wide bare caller (`Wan2GP/wgp.py`, `Wan2GP/models/wan/any2video.py`, `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/*.py`, preprocessors, plugins that import `shared.utils.*` / `models.*` directly). The fix is the same seam for all of them: preserving the absolute `Wan2GP/` mount path.
2. `shared/` + `postprocessing/` resync honoring `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` bucket decisions.
3. `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py` introduced from upstream HEAD plus its companion preset `Wan2GP/profiles/ltx2_presets/HQ (Res2s).json` (both bucket-(a) per CSV rows 110 and 195). SD-005 "off by default" is satisfied because no `Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2*.json` file is modified to reference `res_2s` — the preset is opt-in only. `Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` aligned to upstream HEAD.
4. Stale-doc wording removed from `README.md:39`, `STRUCTURE.md:22`, `STRUCTURE.md:70`, `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202`, **and** from `docs/WAN2GP_FORK_MIGRATION_PLAN.md` wherever the hard `rg` gate patterns match.

**LTX2 non-negotiable clarified**: The rule "Sprint 2 does NOT touch any files under `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/`" applies to reigh-worker-visible behavior and working-tree content. Adding `res2s.py` is an additive upstream-only new file (bucket a, CSV row 110) — no existing ltx2 file is modified. The fork-side carry commit reproduces existing `models/ltx2/*` bytes from the pre-Sprint-2 baseline; post-cutover, `git diff wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline HEAD -- Wan2GP/models/ltx2/` (excluding the new `res2s.py`) must be empty, proving byte preservation.

**Doc-sweep gate clarified**: Both `rg` commands from the idea must return **zero hits**, full stop. No file may be excluded. This requires rewriting stale-wording lines in `docs/WAN2GP_FORK_MIGRATION_PLAN.md` (lines 45, 47, 103, 114-115) alongside the four originally-listed documentation targets.

**Fork construction strategy (unchanged)**: Build integration branch `reigh-sprint-2` on `banodoco/Wan2GP` whose tree equals `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` everywhere EXCEPT the explicit Sprint 2 delta zones. The submodule pins to that branch tip SHA.

## Phase 0: Preflight

### Step 1: Record baseline tag and capture pre-cutover fingerprint (`reigh-worker/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Verify** working tree is clean (expected untracked only: `docs/WAN2GP_FORK_MIGRATION_PLAN.md`, `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv`, `scripts/live_test/`). Confirm HEAD is current `main`.
2. **Create annotated tag** `wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline` on reigh-worker HEAD: `git tag -a wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline -m "Sprint 2 baseline"`. Do NOT push.
3. **Fingerprint ltx2 zone** for byte-preservation verification: `git ls-tree -r HEAD Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ | sha256sum > /tmp/ltx2_pre_cutover.sha256`. Also capture the full tree hash: `git ls-tree -r HEAD Wan2GP | sha256sum > /tmp/wan2gp_pre_cutover.sha256`.
4. **Verify fork access**: confirm `banodoco/Wan2GP` is reachable, capture `main` tip SHA + upstream `deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP` HEAD SHA.

## Phase 1: Construct the `reigh-sprint-2` fork branch

### Step 2: Clone the banodoco fork into a scratch directory (`/tmp/wan2gp-fork/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Clone**: `git clone https://github.com/banodoco/Wan2GP.git /tmp/wan2gp-fork` and `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork fetch origin main`.
2. **Branch from `main`**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork checkout -b reigh-sprint-2 origin/main`. Starting point = upstream HEAD (Sprint 1 state).
3. **Confirm** tip SHA matches Sprint 1's fork cut.

### Step 3: Mirror banodoco-drift onto `reigh-sprint-2` (byte-preserving carry commit)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Intent**: the fork branch's tree must equal `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` as-of baseline for every path (defaults/, models/ltx2/, models/wan/, models/qwen/, loras_qwen/, profiles/, plugins/, preprocessing/, shared/, postprocessing/, top-level configs). This preserves all banodoco drift including the `models/ltx2/` hot zone — bytes only, no review or transformation.
2. **Non-negotiable reconciliation**: the fork's carry commit will write bytes for `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/*` on the fork repository, but those bytes are **byte-identical** to the pre-Sprint-2 reigh-worker baseline. The Sprint 2 no-touch rule governs reigh-worker behavior: post-cutover `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/models/ltx2/*` bytes must equal `wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline` bytes. Step 6.3 below verifies this.
3. **Rsync copy**: from `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` into `/tmp/wan2gp-fork/`, excluding `.git`, `__pycache__`, and anything already gitignored by the fork's own `.gitignore`. Preserve the fork's existing `.gitignore` from upstream (don't overwrite).
4. **Stage + inspect**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork add -A` then `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork status`. Spot-check that the staged delta matches expected banodoco drift: ~22 `models/ltx2/` file diffs, banodoco-only files in `defaults/` / `loras_qwen/` / `plugins/`, etc.
5. **Commit**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork commit -m "banodoco carry: mirror reigh-worker/Wan2GP baseline onto fork"`.

### Step 4: Apply Sprint 2 resync delta on top of the carry commit
**Scope:** Medium
1. **`shared/` triage application** — for every `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` row with path under `Wan2GP/shared/`:
   - **Bucket (a) `accept-upstream`**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork checkout origin/main -- <path>`. Revert to upstream. (Upstream-only additions that are bucket-a and under `shared/` are already in `origin/main`; the carry commit may or may not have touched them depending on whether they existed in reigh-worker pre-Sprint-2 — use `git checkout origin/main --` unconditionally.)
   - **Bucket (b) `upstreamable`**: leave as carry commit (banodoco content preserved).
   - **Bucket (c) `banodoco-fork-or-banodoco-patch`**: leave as carry commit.
   - **Bucket (d) `cruft`**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork rm <path>`. (None expected in `shared/`; verify by grepping CSV.)
2. **`postprocessing/` triage application**: the CSV has zero `Wan2GP/postprocessing/` rows, which means Sprint 1 triage confirmed no drift vs upstream. Verify: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork diff origin/main -- postprocessing/` must be empty. **If drift is found, HALT Sprint 2** and extend the triage CSV with proper bucket classifications before proceeding. Do NOT auto-classify.
3. **`shared/utils/self_refiner.py` alignment**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork checkout origin/main -- shared/utils/self_refiner.py`. Per plan §2c, drift-only resync to upstream HEAD. The CSV does not have an explicit self_refiner row; the plan doc's §2c prose is the authoritative classification (bucket a, drift-only).
4. **`models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py` introduction**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork checkout origin/main -- models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py`. CSV row 110 — bucket (a), upstream-only addition. The file does NOT currently exist in the reigh-worker vendored tree (verified: no `res2s` or `Res2s` match anywhere under `Wan2GP/`). Adding it is a net-new file under `models/ltx2/`, which is permitted under the SD-001 non-negotiable reading (no existing ltx2 file is modified).
5. **`profiles/ltx2_presets/HQ (Res2s).json` introduction**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork checkout origin/main -- "profiles/ltx2_presets/HQ (Res2s).json"`. CSV row 195 — bucket (a), upstream-only addition, paired companion to `res2s.py`. Without this preset the sampler has no UI entrypoint and would be dead code. SD-005 off-by-default is satisfied because no `Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2*.json` file is modified to set `res_2s` as the default sampler; the preset is opt-in only (users must explicitly select it). Verify by grepping upstream's `defaults/ltx2*.json` for any `res_2s` references — expected: none after the carry commit. If a default ltx2 config on upstream already defaults to `res_2s`, **HALT** and re-plan: that would violate SD-005.
6. **Commit the Sprint 2 delta**: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork commit -m "sprint-2: resync shared/ + postprocessing/, align self_refiner, introduce res2s + HQ preset"`. Verify with `git show --stat HEAD` that the diff is confined to paths under `Wan2GP/shared/`, `Wan2GP/postprocessing/`, `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py`, and `Wan2GP/profiles/ltx2_presets/HQ (Res2s).json`.
7. **Pause for user confirmation before pushing** `reigh-sprint-2` to `banodoco/Wan2GP` (visible-to-banodoco action). After confirmation: `git -C /tmp/wan2gp-fork push -u origin reigh-sprint-2`.
8. **Capture tip SHA** for the submodule pointer.

## Phase 2: Reigh-worker submodule cutover

### Step 5: Replace flat `Wan2GP/` tree with submodule (`reigh-worker/.gitmodules`, `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Remove tracked flat tree**: `git rm -r --cached Wan2GP/` (removes from index, leaves working copy). Then `rm -rf Wan2GP/` to remove the physical directory.
2. **Add submodule**: `git submodule add -b reigh-sprint-2 https://github.com/banodoco/Wan2GP.git Wan2GP`. Creates `.gitmodules`, populates `.git/modules/Wan2GP/`, checks out branch tip into `Wan2GP/`.
3. **Pin to the specific SHA** from Step 4.8: `git -C Wan2GP checkout <sha>` then `git add Wan2GP .gitmodules`.
4. **Verify `.gitmodules`**:
   ```
   [submodule "Wan2GP"]
       path = Wan2GP
       url = https://github.com/banodoco/Wan2GP.git
       branch = reigh-sprint-2
   ```
5. **Verify submodule health**: `git submodule status` shows `Wan2GP` at the pinned SHA with no `+`/`-` prefix.
6. **Broader caller note**: no changes required to `source/core/runtime_paths.py` — absolute `Wan2GP/` on `sys.path` continues to satisfy every bare-import caller across the tree (the 3 named in `vendor_imports.py:76-88` plus all vendored-tree internal callers in `Wan2GP/wgp.py`, `Wan2GP/models/wan/*`, `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/*`, preprocessors, plugins). The 26+ dotted `Wan2GP.*` callers remain supported because the repo root (containing `Wan2GP/`) is also on `sys.path` during normal bootstrap.
7. **Do not commit yet** — run validation gates first.

### Step 6: Validate path-contract + filesystem mount (`reigh-worker/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Strict path-contract one-liner** (filesystem-only per plan §2i; safe in any Python env):
   ```bash
   python3 -c "import os, sys; from source.core.runtime_paths import ensure_wan2gp_on_path, get_wan2gp_path; ensure_wan2gp_on_path(); assert os.path.abspath(str(get_wan2gp_path())) in [os.path.abspath(p) for p in sys.path]; assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_handler.py'); assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_main.py'); assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/shared/utils/loras_mutipliers.py'); assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py'); assert os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py'); print('path-contract smoke OK')"
   ```
   Must exit 0 with `path-contract smoke OK`.
2. **Real unmocked bridge-import smoke** (runs in the worker venv where SciPy is available, per plan §2i caveat about `shared.utils.__init__.py:1-3`):
   ```bash
   uv run --python 3.10 python -c "from source.runtime.wgp_ports.vendor_imports import get_qwen_family_handler, get_shared_lora_utils_module, get_qwen_main_module; get_qwen_family_handler(); get_shared_lora_utils_module(); get_qwen_main_module(); print('bridge imports OK')"
   ```
   This is the real filesystem-backed exercise of the three bare-module callers, closing the gap that monkeypatched bridge-contract tests leave open.
3. **LTX2 byte-preservation check**: `git diff wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline -- Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ | grep -v '^+++\|^---\|res2s.py'` must be empty (the only expected new content under `models/ltx2/` is the new file `res2s.py`). Equivalent stricter form: `git diff wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline -- Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ | head` shows only additions for `res2s.py`.

### Step 7: Run the 6-file travel regression suite + bridge-contract regressions (`tests/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **6-file travel suite**:
   ```bash
   uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_travel_guidance_config.py tests/test_travel_ltx_vpt.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_travel.py tests/test_ltx_hybrid_vgkfi.py tests/test_travel_payload_contracts.py tests/test_travel_orchestrator_terminal_gating.py
   ```
   All green. Indirect coverage: travel orchestrator → wgp_patches → vendor_imports, so the suite does exercise the import seams in a real end-to-end path.
2. **Supplementary bridge-contract regressions** (to close the mocked-only verification gap):
   ```bash
   uv run --python 3.10 pytest tests/test_wgp_bridge_contracts.py tests/test_runtime_registry_readonly_contracts.py
   ```
   Must be all green. Even though these tests partially monkeypatch `ensure_wan2gp_on_path`, running them post-cutover verifies the bridge API surface hasn't regressed.
3. **On failure**: inspect whether the resynced `shared/` file touches travel-path code. If yes, reclassify the triggering file to bucket (c) on the fork branch, amend Step 4.1, re-push, re-pin. Do NOT silently widen the resync.

## Phase 3: Doc updates

### Step 8: Rewrite stale vendored wording (`reigh-worker/README.md`, `reigh-worker/STRUCTURE.md`, `reigh-worker/docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md`, `reigh-worker/docs/WAN2GP_FORK_MIGRATION_PLAN.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **`README.md:39`** — replace "vendored upstream input ... subtree" wording with submodule-accurate language describing pointer bumps and mirroring upstream requirement changes into root worker metadata before regenerating `uv.lock`.
2. **`README.md:103`** (Powered By section, if present) — correct "the `Wan2GP/` directory contains the upstream engine" to note the submodule pinned to `banodoco/Wan2GP`.
3. **`STRUCTURE.md:22`** — replace `vendored upstream. Dependency drift ...` with submodule-accurate language describing pointer bumps rather than subtree edits.
4. **`STRUCTURE.md:70`** — replace `Upstream video generation engine (vendored, DO NOT MODIFY)` with `Git submodule pointing at banodoco/Wan2GP; edit via fork PR + pointer bump, not in-place`.
5. **`docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202`** — replace `still a vendored upstream subtree` with submodule language; preserve the paragraph's existing guidance about mirroring requirements into root worker metadata.
6. **`docs/WAN2GP_FORK_MIGRATION_PLAN.md` — required rewrites so both hard `rg` gates pass**:
   - **Line 45**: change `as a vendored upstream subtree` → `as a flat vendored tree prior to Sprint 2` (removes `subtree` adjacency).
   - **Line 47**: change `"Upstream video generation engine (vendored, DO NOT MODIFY)."` → `"Upstream video generation engine (vendored, do-not-edit-in-place)."` (case-sensitive `rg` treats lowercase differently; `DO NOT MODIFY` disappears).
   - **Line 103**: change `to remove stale vendored-subtree wording` → `to remove pre-Sprint-2 vendored-tree wording` (removes `subtree`).
   - **Lines 114-115 (regex-reproduction block)**: replace the literal `rg "vendored.*subtree"` and `rg -H -n 'vendor' {}` lines with a prose pointer that does not self-match. Concretely, rewrite the block to:
     > Doc-sweep gate: run the two regex sweeps specified in the Sprint 2 brief (gate C). The first pattern looks for the adjective `vendored` co-occurring with the noun describing a `subtree`-style import on the same line. The second reads every file in `docs/`, `README.md`, `STRUCTURE.md` that mentions `Wan2GP` and matches the uppercase "do-not-edit" notice phrase. Both sweeps must return zero hits.

     The key is that this block must NOT contain the string `vendored` followed by `subtree` on any single line, and must NOT contain the uppercase phrase `DO NOT MODIFY` anywhere. Verify line-by-line with `rg "vendored.*subtree" docs/WAN2GP_FORK_MIGRATION_PLAN.md` and `rg 'DO NOT MODIFY' docs/WAN2GP_FORK_MIGRATION_PLAN.md` — both must return zero hits on the rewritten file.
7. **Track `docs/WAN2GP_FORK_MIGRATION_PLAN.md` in git as part of Sprint 2**: since it's currently untracked (per `git status` at baseline), and Sprint 2 now depends on its content passing the gate, stage it in the Sprint 2 commit alongside the other doc updates.

### Step 9: Doc-sweep gate — ZERO hits required (`reigh-worker/docs/`, `reigh-worker/README.md`, `reigh-worker/STRUCTURE.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Narrow sweep** — must return exactly zero lines:
   ```bash
   rg "vendored.*subtree" docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md
   ```
2. **Broader sweep** — must return exactly zero lines:
   ```bash
   rg -l 'Wan2GP' docs/ README.md STRUCTURE.md | xargs -I{} rg -H -n 'DO NOT MODIFY' {}
   ```
3. **Zero exceptions**. If either sweep returns any line, Sprint 2 is not done. Fix the matching line and re-run. No "historical prose" exemption — all four doc surfaces (including `docs/WAN2GP_FORK_MIGRATION_PLAN.md`) must pass cleanly.

## Phase 4: Land

### Step 10: Commit the submodule cutover + doc updates (`reigh-worker/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Stage**: `.gitmodules`, `Wan2GP` (the gitlink), all deletions under `Wan2GP/`, `README.md`, `STRUCTURE.md`, `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md`, `docs/WAN2GP_FORK_MIGRATION_PLAN.md`.
2. **Commit** with a message like `chore: replace vendored Wan2GP tree with submodule at reigh-sprint-2`. Reference the baseline tag in the body.
3. **Do not push without explicit user confirmation** — the submodule commit permanently alters the repo shape.

## Execution Order

1. Preflight + baseline tag (Step 1) — rollback anchor.
2. Build the fork branch offline in a scratch clone (Steps 2–4). Pause for user confirmation before the push in Step 4.7.
3. Cut reigh-worker over to the submodule (Step 5), then gate on the cheap filesystem smoke (Step 6.1).
4. Real unmocked bridge smoke (Step 6.2) and LTX2 byte-preservation check (Step 6.3).
5. Travel suite + bridge-contract tests (Step 7).
6. Doc rewrites (Step 8) and zero-hit doc-sweep gate (Step 9).
7. Commit locally (Step 10). Do not push.

## Validation Order

1. Cheapest: strict path-contract smoke (Step 6.1).
2. Fast: LTX2 byte-preservation diff (Step 6.3), real bridge smoke (Step 6.2), doc-sweep regex gates (Step 9).
3. Slowest: 6-file travel suite + bridge-contract pytest (Step 7).

Rollback if ANY hard gate fails (path-contract smoke, bridge-import smoke, 6-file suite, bridge-contract tests, LTX2 byte-preservation, or doc-sweep gate):
```bash
git submodule deinit Wan2GP && git reset --hard wan2gp-mig-sprint-2-baseline
```
The banodoco `reigh-sprint-2` branch stays in place after rollback (no force-delete) so retry does not have to rebuild it.

        Known accepted debt grouped by subsystem:
        {
  "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-007",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-008",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-018",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-106",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-107",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "are-the-success-criteria-well-prioritized-and-verifiable": [

  "did-the-work-fully-address-the-issue-hints-user-notes-and-approved-plan-requirements": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-013",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the package is internally inconsistent. the plan body says to keep `activepairdata` in `usestate` and make phase 2 optional, but the attached metadata and success criteria still describe the previous derive-via-`usememo` / remove-`setactivepairdata` / simplify-`onpairclick` plan. that means the approved-plan requirements are only partially coherent as presented.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-108",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-110",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-109",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],

        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"

      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-008",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]

      {
        "id": "DEBT-018",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]

      {
        "id": "DEBT-106",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-107",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      }
    ]

- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
